# WILD KARAKORAM — Video Prompts

10 seconds per beat · image-to-video · every prompt standalone · each beat has a plain Description and a Stability line to stop the animals morphing · each beat continues from the one before (see Continuity)

**Beat 1**
[Script Segment Text]: "A ledge no wider than a hand, three hundred meters above the valley floor. And standing on it,"
Description: The camera rises up the cliff to reveal a goat on a hand-wide ledge. Where: The Karakoram mountains of northern Pakistan. Light: warm late-afternoon summer light. Shot: long range, aerial shot rising fast up the cliff face, then slowing as the ibex comes into frame. In the clip: the camera climbs the wall and the ibex holds perfectly still, while wisps of cloud stream past the rock below her.
Video Prompt: A vast sheer granite wall rising out of a deep valley, and high on it a tiny ledge where a female Himalayan ibex with a light sandy-grey coat, short curved horns and a slim, agile build stands perfectly still, three hundred meters of air beneath her, in the Karakoram mountains of northern Pakistan. Long range: aerial view from high above, the animals small against the immense walls. Over 10 seconds, the camera climbs the wall and the ibex holds perfectly still; wisps of cloud stream past the rock below her. Camera movement: Aerial shot rising fast up the cliff face, then slowing as the ibex comes into frame. Lighting: warm low late-afternoon summer sun, golden rim light on fur and horns, long shadows across the rock. Mood: awe. Keep every animal exactly as in the starting image: same body shape, four legs, horns, coat and markings, and the same number of animals for the whole clip; no morphing, melting, stretching, flickering, extra limbs or new animals appearing; one smooth continuous shot with no cuts or sudden zooms. Visual style: photorealistic landmark wildlife documentary footage, 16:9 widescreen, granite and slate cliffs, glaciers and high peaks, natural gently warm grade, very shallow depth of field with creamy bokeh, ultra-fine micro-detail. Smooth, steady camera with gentle natural lens breathing and focus pulls; leaps and chases in true high-frame-rate slow motion; realistic animal anatomy, weight and locomotion, natural physics for rock, snow and wind. Real wildlife documentary footage, as filmed by a natural-history camera crew from hidden positions, mixing long-range telephoto, aerial and close-range remote cameras: the animals are wild, unposed and unaware of the camera, behaving exactly as they would in nature, with true-to-life anatomy, proportions, fur, wool and horn texture, natural colors, subtle atmospheric haze over distance, fine natural film grain and the slight imperfections of real footage. No CGI, cartoon, fantasy or illustrated look, no exaggerated poses or expressions, no oversaturation. No text, no logos, no music, no human presence, villages or vehicles, no gore.
Shot Range: Long range
Camera Movement: Aerial shot rising fast up the cliff face, then slowing as the ibex comes into frame
